A scheme is the\n38: // foundation for a versioned API and versioned configuration over time.\n39: //\n40: // In a Scheme, a Type is a particular Go struct, a Version is a point-in-time\n41: // identifier for a particular representation of that Type (typically backwards\n42: // compatible), a Kind is the unique name for that Type within the Version, and a\n43: // Group identifies a set of Versions, Kinds, and Types that evolve over time. CRD Lifecycle Analysis: Cross-Kubernetes Ecosystem\n\n## Files Examined\n\n### apiextensions-apiserver: Type Definitions & Server-Side Lifecycle\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apis/apiextensions/types.go` — Internal hub type definitions for CustomResourceDefinition, CustomResourceDefinitionSpec, CustomResourceValidation\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/register.go` — External v1 API types and Scheme registration for CustomResourceDefinition and ConversionReview\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/types.go` — v1-versioned type definitions with versioning logic\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apiserver/customresource_handler.go` — Dynamic HTTP handler that processes CustomResource requests at runtime\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/registry/customresource/etcd.go` — Storage backend implementation using etcd via generic REST storage\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/registry/customresource/strategy.go` — Storage strategy for validation, field handling, and etcd predicates\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/registry/customresourcedefinition/strategy.go` — CRD-specific storage strategy for the CRD resource itself\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/crdserverscheme/crdserverscheme.go` — Scheme for serving CRD types with unstructured representation\n\n### client-go: Client-Side Access Layer\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/dynamic/simple.go` — DynamicClient wrapping rest.Interface for schema-less access to any resource\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/dynamic/dynamicinformer/informer.go` — DynamicSharedInformerFactory and DynamicInformer for watching CRs with ListWatch pattern\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/informers/generic.go` — GenericInformer interface and genericInformer implementation wrapping SharedIndexInformer and cache.GenericLister\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/dynamic/dynamiclister/lister.go` — Generic listers for filtering cached CRs from the informer cache\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/typed/apiextensions/v1/customresourcedefinition.go` — Typed client for CustomResourceDefinition resources (for managing CRDs themselves)\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/rest/client.go` — Base RESTClient providing HTTP semantics for all client operations\n\n### apimachinery: Foundation Types & Runtime System\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/scheme.go` — Scheme type registry mapping GVK ↔ Go struct types, manages type conversion and serialization\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/schema/group_version.go` — GroupVersionKind and GroupVersionResource definitions for identifying resource types\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1/unstructured/unstructured.go` — Unstructured type (map[string]interface{}) enabling schema-less object representation\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1/types.go` — TypeMeta and ObjectMeta with standard API fields (apiVersion, kind, name, namespace, etc.)\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/serializer/` — Codec factories for encoding/decoding objects in JSON, CBOR, protobuf\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/interfaces.go` — ObjectTyper, Encoder, Decoder interfaces defining the type system boundaries\n\n### api: Type Registration & Hub Types\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/api/` — Contains no CRD types directly, but core/v1 defines ObjectMeta and standard API group types\n- Type registration happens through SchemeBuilder pattern (AddToScheme functions)\n- Serves as the authoritative source for API group structures across the ecosystem\n\n---\n\n## Dependency Chain\n\n### 1. **Foundation: apimachinery Types**\n\nThe entire CRD ecosystem is built on foundational types defined in apimachinery:\n\n#### Scheme (runtime.Scheme)\n- Central registry maintaining bidirectional mappings:\n  - `GVK → reflect.Type` (what Go type represents this Kind?)\n  - `reflect.Type → []GVK` (what versions does this type have?)\n- Manages serialization codecs for objects\n- Enables runtime type discovery and conversion\n- Example: `scheme.AddKnownTypes(GroupVersion, &CustomResourceDefinition{})`\n\n#### GroupVersionKind (GVK) & GroupVersionResource (GVR)\n- **GVK**: Identifies the *data format* (apiVersion + kind) → `apiextensions.k8s.io/v1 CustomResourceDefinition`\n- **GVR**: Identifies the *API endpoint* (group + version + plural resource name) → `apiextensions.k8s.io/v1/customresourcedefinitions`\n- Schema provides conversion: `GVK.GroupVersion().WithResource(resource)` → `GVR`\n\n#### ObjectMeta & TypeMeta\n- **TypeMeta**: Contains `apiVersion` and `kind` fields\n- **ObjectMeta**: Contains standard metadata (name, namespace, labels, annotations, uid, resourceVersion, etc.)\n- All Kubernetes objects embed both via composition\n\n#### Unstructured Type\n- `map[string]interface{}` wrapper enabling schema-less representation\n- Implements `runtime.Object`, `metav1.Object`, `metav1.ListInterface`\n- Key for dynamic clients: they work with Unstructured, not typed structs\n- Serialization: JSON → map unmarshaling, map → JSON marshaling\n\n---\n\n### 2. **Type Definitions: apiextensions-apiserver Types**\n\nCRD type definitions are split across internal (hub) and versioned (external) representations:\n\n#### Internal Hub Types\n- **Location**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/types.go`\n- **Purpose**: The canonical representation used internally for conversion and validation\n- **Key Types**:\n  - `CustomResourceDefinition`: Spec contains group, names, scope, validation schema, versions\n  - `CustomResourceDefinitionSpec`: Describes what a custom resource looks like\n  - `CustomResourceValidation`: JSONSchemaProps defining structural schema for CRs\n  - `CustomResourceDefinitionStatus`: Conditions tracking CRD establishment, acceptance, etc.\n- **Registration**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/register.go` adds these to internal GroupVersion\n\n#### External v1 Types\n- **Location**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/types.go`\n- **Purpose**: User-facing API surface with versioning guarantees\n- **Registration**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/register.go` registers to SchemeGroupVersion\n- **SchemeBuilder Pattern**:\n  ```go\n  var (\n    SchemeBuilder      = runtime.NewSchemeBuilder(addKnownTypes, addDefaultingFuncs)\n    localSchemeBuilder = &SchemeBuilder\n    AddToScheme        = localSchemeBuilder.AddToScheme\n  )\n\n  func addKnownTypes(scheme *runtime.Scheme) error {\n    scheme.AddKnownTypes(SchemeGroupVersion,\n      &CustomResourceDefinition{},\n      &CustomResourceDefinitionList{},\n      &ConversionReview{},\n    )\n    metav1.AddToGroupVersion(scheme, SchemeGroupVersion)\n    return nil\n  }\n  ```\n\n#### Scheme Integration Points\n1. apiextensions-apiserver initializes a scheme in `crdserverscheme.go`\n2. Typed client (client-go) has its own scheme registration for CRDs\n3. Dynamic client uses unstructured scheme, bypassing typed registration\n\n---\n\n### 3. **Server-Side Lifecycle: Validation → Storage → HTTP Handler**\n\n#### Validation Layer\n- **Location**: `pkg/apiserver/validation/` and `pkg/registry/customresourcedefinition/strategy.go`\n- **Process**:\n  1. CRD Spec contains JSONSchemaProps defining CustomResource validation rules\n  2. On CR Create/Update, apiextensions-apiserver validates against the CRD's schema\n  3. Validation is structural: object properties, type checking, required fields\n  4. Conversion webhooks (in ConversionStrategy) transform CRs between versions\n\n#### etcd Storage Layer\n- **Location**: `pkg/registry/customresource/etcd.go` and `strategy.go`\n- **Pattern**: Generic etcd backend provided by apiserver framework\n- **Key Mechanism**:\n  ```go\n  // In etcd.go\n  type REST struct {\n    *genericregistry.Store  // Wraps generic etcd storage\n  }\n\n  // Storage key path in etcd: /customresources/{group}/{version}/{kind}/{namespace}/{name}\n  ```\n- **Storage Strategy**: Implements predicates for field/label filtering, name validation\n- **Serialization**: Objects stored as JSON in etcd, deserialized to Unstructured on retrieval\n\n#### Dynamic HTTP Handler\n- **Location**: `pkg/apiserver/customresource_handler.go`\n- **Purpose**: Serves `/apis/{group}/{version}/{resource}` endpoints for all CRs\n- **Architecture**:\n  1. `crdHandler` filters HTTP requests by path and routes to appropriate storage\n  2. For each CRD, maintains a `crdStorage` map with a REST storage instance\n  3. On CRD change (add/update/delete), handler rebuilds storage map\n  4. Handles all CRUD operations: Get, List, Watch, Create, Update, Delete, Patch\n- **Key Pattern**: Handler is registered as a filter, never collides with explicitly registered endpoints\n- **Serialization**: Handles content negotiation (JSON, CBOR, protobuf) via NegotiatedSerializer\n\n#### Discovery & OpenAPI\n- **Endpoint**: `/openapi/v3` provides OpenAPI schema for all resources including CRs\n- **Generated**: `pkg/controller/openapi/controller.go` watches CRDs and updates OpenAPI spec\n- **Discovery**: `/api` and `/apis/{group}` endpoints list available resources\n\n---\n\n### 4. **Client-Side Access Layer: Typed Client → Informer → Lister → Dynamic Client**\n\n#### RESTClient Foundation\n- **Location**: `client-go/rest/client.go`\n- **Role**: Low-level HTTP client\n- **Responsibilities**:\n  - Request building (URL, headers, query params)\n  - Authentication (kubeconfig, serviceaccount tokens)\n  - Transport handling (TLS, keep-alive)\n  - Response unmarshaling\n\n#### Typed Client (for CRD Management)\n- **Generated Location**: `client-go/kubernetes/typed/apiextensions/v1/`\n- **Purpose**: Typed access to CustomResourceDefinition resources themselves\n- **Example**: `clientset.ApiextensionsV1().CustomResourceDefinitions().Create(crd)`\n- **Type Safety**: Uses Go structs (CustomResourceDefinition) with compile-time checking\n- **Scheme Usage**: Depends on apimachinery's Scheme for type registration\n\n#### Dynamic Client (for Custom Resources)\n- **Location**: `client-go/dynamic/simple.go`\n- **Wraps**: `rest.Interface` (RESTClient)\n- **Uses**: `Unstructured` type for all objects\n- **Key Properties**:\n  - Schema-less: No compile-time type registration required\n  - GVR-based: Identifies resources by GroupVersionResource, not Scheme\n  - Works immediately with CRs without code generation\n- **Example**:\n  ```go\n  client := dynamic.NewForConfig(config)\n  resource := schema.GroupVersionResource{\n    Group: \"example.com\",\n    Version: \"v1\",\n    Resource: \"widgets\",\n  }\n  widgets := client.Resource(resource).Namespace(\"default\")\n  ```\n\n#### Informers & Listers (Client-Side Cache)\n- **Location**: `client-go/informers/` and `client-go/dynamic/dynamicinformer/`\n- **Purpose**: Local in-memory cache of resources with watch support\n\n##### SharedIndexInformer Flow\n1. **ListWatch Construction** (`dynamicinformer/informer.go:150-176`):\n   - ListFunc: `client.Resource(gvr).Namespace(ns).List(ctx, options)`\n   - WatchFunc: `client.Resource(gvr).Namespace(ns).Watch(ctx, options)`\n   - These funcs delegate to DynamicClient → RESTClient\n\n2. **Caching** (`tools/cache/shared_informer.go`):\n   - Stores objects in thread-safe Indexers\n   - Listwatch periodically lists all objects (resync), watches for changes\n   - Objects cached as Unstructured\n\n3. **Event Handling**:\n   - Informer maintains a queue of Add/Update/Delete events\n   - Calls registered eventhandlers for each event\n   - Indexers updated in response\n\n##### Lister Pattern\n- **Location**: `client-go/dynamic/dynamiclister/`\n- **Purpose**: Query interface over the informer cache\n- **Operations**:\n  - `List(selector)`: Get all objects matching label selector from cache\n  - `Get(name)`: Get single object from cache\n  - `ByNamespace(ns)`: Restrict to specific namespace\n- **Type**: `cache.GenericLister` interface works with Unstructured objects\n\n##### GenericInformer Interface\n- **Location**: `client-go/informers/generic.go`\n- **Definition**:\n  ```go\n  type GenericInformer interface {\n    Informer() cache.SharedIndexInformer\n    Lister() cache.GenericLister\n  }\n  ```\n- **Usage**: Abstracts over typed and dynamic informers with same interface\n\n#### DynamicSharedInformerFactory\n- **Location**: `client-go/dynamic/dynamicinformer/informer.go`\n- **Purpose**: Multiplexes informers for multiple CRDs\n- **Key Method**: `ForResource(gvr)` → `GenericInformer` for that GVR\n- **Lifecycle**:\n  1. Create factory: `NewDynamicSharedInformerFactory(client, resync)`\n  2. Get informer: `factory.ForResource(gvr).Informer()`\n  3. Start: `factory.Start(stopCh)` launches watchers in goroutines\n  4. Wait sync: `factory.WaitForCacheSync(stopCh)` ensures all caches populated\n  5. Query: `factory.ForResource(gvr).Lister().List(selector)`\n\n---\n\n## Analysis: How the Sub-Projects Integrate\n\n### Cross-Project Dependency Flow\n\n```\napimachinery (Foundation)\n    ↓ (defines core types)\n    ├→ Scheme, GVK/GVR, ObjectMeta, Unstructured, Codec interfaces\n\napiextensions-apiserver (Server-Side)\n    ↓ (depends on apimachinery)\n    ├→ Extends Scheme with CustomResourceDefinition types\n    ├→ Uses Unstructured for storing arbitrary CRs\n    ├→ Validates CRs against CRD schemas\n    ├→ Stores in etcd using generic REST storage\n    └→ Serves HTTP endpoints for CRUD + Watch\n\nclient-go (Client-Side)\n    ↓ (depends on apimachinery + apiextensions for CRD types)\n    ├→ Provides RESTClient for HTTP requests\n    ├→ Typed client for CustomResourceDefinition management\n    ├→ Dynamic client for CustomResource access (Unstructured)\n    ├→ Informers for caching with ListWatch\n    └→ Listers for querying cache\n\napi (Hub Types)\n    ↓ (authoritative definitions)\n    └→ Provides standard ObjectMeta, TypeMeta, etc.\n```\n\n### The Bridge: Unstructured Representation\n\nThe critical innovation enabling the CRD architecture:\n1. **On Server**: CRDs define validation via JSONSchemaProps, but CRs stored as Unstructured JSON in etcd\n2. **On Wire**: Objects transmitted as JSON with apiVersion/kind in TypeMeta\n3. **On Client**: DynamicClient receives Unstructured objects, can query/manipulate without schema knowledge\n4. **Checkpoint**: Validation happens server-side before storage, client-side type checking is optional\n\n### Scheme Registration Patterns\n\n**Typed CRD Client**:\n```go\n// In apiextensions-apiserver and client-go\nscheme := runtime.NewScheme()\napiextensionsv1.AddToScheme(scheme)  // Registers CustomResourceDefinition, ConversionReview types\n```\n\n**Dynamic Client**:\n```go\n// In client-go/dynamic\nconfig := ConfigFor(inConfig)\nconfig.NegotiatedSerializer = newBasicNegotiatedSerializer()  // Unstructured codec only\n// No Scheme registration needed!\n```\n\n### The Informer Checkpoint\n\nWhen a DynamicInformer watches a CRD's CRs:\n1. **Server-Side**: customresource_handler watches etcd, returns Unstructured objects\n2. **Over Wire**: HTTP/Watch stream delivers serialized Unstructured (as JSON)\n3. **Client-Side**: DynamicClient deserializes to Unstructured\n4. **In Cache**: Informer's Indexers store Unstructured objects\n5. **Query Time**: Lister returns Unstructured from cache\n\nNo type conversion happens; objects remain Unstructured throughout the pipeline.\n\n### Why Four Sub-Projects?\n\n1. **apimachinery**: Stable foundation used by all Kubernetes code; changes rarely break compatibility\n2. **apiextensions-apiserver**: Can evolve CRD API without breaking apimachinery consumers\n3. **client-go**: Isolated client library; can be vendored independently with different versions than apiserver\n4. **api**: Core and extension API definitions; separates type definitions from implementation\n\nEach project has its own versioning, release cycle, and import constraints. This modularity allows:\n- Users can update client-go independently from their cluster version\n- New CRD features don't require updating all consumers\n- Clear separation of concerns: definitions (api) vs runtime (apiextensions) vs client libraries (client-go)\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\nA Custom Resource Definition flows through the Kubernetes ecosystem as follows:\n\n**Server-side**: A CRD type definition (stored as a CustomResourceDefinition object) is validated, stored in etcd, and served via the dynamic HTTP handler which creates a REST endpoint for custom resources. The endpoint validates CRs against the CRD's schema and stores them as Unstructured JSON objects in etcd.\n\n**Client-side**: Clients access custom resources via the DynamicClient, which performs HTTP requests to the CRD-provided endpoints. A DynamicSharedInformerFactory establishes ListWatch connections, caching Unstructured objects locally and maintaining an in-memory index. Listers query this cache using the GenericInformer interface, enabling operator code to efficiently watch and react to CR changes without compile-time knowledge of the resource type.\n\n**Cross-cutting**: The Scheme registry in apimachinery ties typed and untyped clients together, while GroupVersionKind/GroupVersionResource identifiers enable routing and discovery. The Unstructured type allows the entire pipeline to work with schema-less objects, making CRDs extensible without requiring code generation for each new resource type.\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":12,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3495,"cache_read_input_tokens":79410,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3495},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"bb201810-ae7e-4561-9c77-da6f1a039b15","uuid":"70ee4f0e-ad82-4bbf-90b0-bc13c3e0f70f"}

CRD Lifecycle Analysis: Cross-Kubernetes Ecosystem\n\n## Files Examined\n\n### apiextensions-apiserver: Type Definitions & Server-Side Lifecycle\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apis/apiextensions/types.go` — Internal hub type definitions for CustomResourceDefinition, CustomResourceDefinitionSpec, CustomResourceValidation\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/register.go` — External v1 API types and Scheme registration for CustomResourceDefinition and ConversionReview\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/types.go` — v1-versioned type definitions with versioning logic\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/apiserver/customresource_handler.go` — Dynamic HTTP handler that processes CustomResource requests at runtime\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/registry/customresource/etcd.go` — Storage backend implementation using etcd via generic REST storage\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/registry/customresource/strategy.go` — Storage strategy for validation, field handling, and etcd predicates\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/registry/customresourcedefinition/strategy.go` — CRD-specific storage strategy for the CRD resource itself\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apiextensions-apiserver/pkg/crdserverscheme/crdserverscheme.go` — Scheme for serving CRD types with unstructured representation\n\n### client-go: Client-Side Access Layer\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/dynamic/simple.go` — DynamicClient wrapping rest.Interface for schema-less access to any resource\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/dynamic/dynamicinformer/informer.go` — DynamicSharedInformerFactory and DynamicInformer for watching CRs with ListWatch pattern\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/informers/generic.go` — GenericInformer interface and genericInformer implementation wrapping SharedIndexInformer and cache.GenericLister\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/dynamic/dynamiclister/lister.go` — Generic listers for filtering cached CRs from the informer cache\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/kubernetes/typed/apiextensions/v1/customresourcedefinition.go` — Typed client for CustomResourceDefinition resources (for managing CRDs themselves)\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/client-go/rest/client.go` — Base RESTClient providing HTTP semantics for all client operations\n\n### apimachinery: Foundation Types & Runtime System\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/scheme.go` — Scheme type registry mapping GVK ↔ Go struct types, manages type conversion and serialization\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/schema/group_version.go` — GroupVersionKind and GroupVersionResource definitions for identifying resource types\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1/unstructured/unstructured.go` — Unstructured type (map[string]interface{}) enabling schema-less object representation\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/apis/meta/v1/types.go` — TypeMeta and ObjectMeta with standard API fields (apiVersion, kind, name, namespace, etc.)\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/serializer/` — Codec factories for encoding/decoding objects in JSON, CBOR, protobuf\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/apimachinery/pkg/runtime/interfaces.go` — ObjectTyper, Encoder, Decoder interfaces defining the type system boundaries\n\n### api: Type Registration & Hub Types\n- `staging/src/k8s.io/api/` — Contains no CRD types directly, but core/v1 defines ObjectMeta and standard API group types\n- Type registration happens through SchemeBuilder pattern (AddToScheme functions)\n- Serves as the authoritative source for API group structures across the ecosystem\n\n---\n\n## Dependency Chain\n\n### 1. **Foundation: apimachinery Types**\n\nThe entire CRD ecosystem is built on foundational types defined in apimachinery:\n\n#### Scheme (runtime.Scheme)\n- Central registry maintaining bidirectional mappings:\n  - `GVK → reflect.Type` (what Go type represents this Kind?)\n  - `reflect.Type → []GVK` (what versions does this type have?)\n- Manages serialization codecs for objects\n- Enables runtime type discovery and conversion\n- Example: `scheme.AddKnownTypes(GroupVersion, &CustomResourceDefinition{})`\n\n#### GroupVersionKind (GVK) & GroupVersionResource (GVR)\n- **GVK**: Identifies the *data format* (apiVersion + kind) → `apiextensions.k8s.io/v1 CustomResourceDefinition`\n- **GVR**: Identifies the *API endpoint* (group + version + plural resource name) → `apiextensions.k8s.io/v1/customresourcedefinitions`\n- Schema provides conversion: `GVK.GroupVersion().WithResource(resource)` → `GVR`\n\n#### ObjectMeta & TypeMeta\n- **TypeMeta**: Contains `apiVersion` and `kind` fields\n- **ObjectMeta**: Contains standard metadata (name, namespace, labels, annotations, uid, resourceVersion, etc.)\n- All Kubernetes objects embed both via composition\n\n#### Unstructured Type\n- `map[string]interface{}` wrapper enabling schema-less representation\n- Implements `runtime.Object`, `metav1.Object`, `metav1.ListInterface`\n- Key for dynamic clients: they work with Unstructured, not typed structs\n- Serialization: JSON → map unmarshaling, map → JSON marshaling\n\n---\n\n### 2. **Type Definitions: apiextensions-apiserver Types**\n\nCRD type definitions are split across internal (hub) and versioned (external) representations:\n\n#### Internal Hub Types\n- **Location**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/types.go`\n- **Purpose**: The canonical representation used internally for conversion and validation\n- **Key Types**:\n  - `CustomResourceDefinition`: Spec contains group, names, scope, validation schema, versions\n  - `CustomResourceDefinitionSpec`: Describes what a custom resource looks like\n  - `CustomResourceValidation`: JSONSchemaProps defining structural schema for CRs\n  - `CustomResourceDefinitionStatus`: Conditions tracking CRD establishment, acceptance, etc.\n- **Registration**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/register.go` adds these to internal GroupVersion\n\n#### External v1 Types\n- **Location**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/types.go`\n- **Purpose**: User-facing API surface with versioning guarantees\n- **Registration**: `pkg/apis/apiextensions/v1/register.go` registers to SchemeGroupVersion\n- **SchemeBuilder Pattern**:\n  ```go\n  var (\n    SchemeBuilder      = runtime.NewSchemeBuilder(addKnownTypes, addDefaultingFuncs)\n    localSchemeBuilder = &SchemeBuilder\n    AddToScheme        = localSchemeBuilder.AddToScheme\n  )\n\n  func addKnownTypes(scheme *runtime.Scheme) error {\n    scheme.AddKnownTypes(SchemeGroupVersion,\n      &CustomResourceDefinition{},\n      &CustomResourceDefinitionList{},\n      &ConversionReview{},\n    )\n    metav1.AddToGroupVersion(scheme, SchemeGroupVersion)\n    return nil\n  }\n  ```\n\n#### Scheme Integration Points\n1. apiextensions-apiserver initializes a scheme in `crdserverscheme.go`\n2. Typed client (client-go) has its own scheme registration for CRDs\n3. Dynamic client uses unstructured scheme, bypassing typed registration\n\n---\n\n### 3. **Server-Side Lifecycle: Validation → Storage → HTTP Handler**\n\n#### Validation Layer\n- **Location**: `pkg/apiserver/validation/` and `pkg/registry/customresourcedefinition/strategy.go`\n- **Process**:\n  1. CRD Spec contains JSONSchemaProps defining CustomResource validation rules\n  2. On CR Create/Update, apiextensions-apiserver validates against the CRD's schema\n  3. Validation is structural: object properties, type checking, required fields\n  4. Conversion webhooks (in ConversionStrategy) transform CRs between versions\n\n#### etcd Storage Layer\n- **Location**: `pkg/registry/customresource/etcd.go` and `strategy.go`\n- **Pattern**: Generic etcd backend provided by apiserver framework\n- **Key Mechanism**:\n  ```go\n  // In etcd.go\n  type REST struct {\n    *genericregistry.Store  // Wraps generic etcd storage\n  }\n\n  // Storage key path in etcd: /customresources/{group}/{version}/{kind}/{namespace}/{name}\n  ```\n- **Storage Strategy**: Implements predicates for field/label filtering, name validation\n- **Serialization**: Objects stored as JSON in etcd, deserialized to Unstructured on retrieval\n\n#### Dynamic HTTP Handler\n- **Location**: `pkg/apiserver/customresource_handler.go`\n- **Purpose**: Serves `/apis/{group}/{version}/{resource}` endpoints for all CRs\n- **Architecture**:\n  1. `crdHandler` filters HTTP requests by path and routes to appropriate storage\n  2. For each CRD, maintains a `crdStorage` map with a REST storage instance\n  3. On CRD change (add/update/delete), handler rebuilds storage map\n  4. Handles all CRUD operations: Get, List, Watch, Create, Update, Delete, Patch\n- **Key Pattern**: Handler is registered as a filter, never collides with explicitly registered endpoints\n- **Serialization**: Handles content negotiation (JSON, CBOR, protobuf) via NegotiatedSerializer\n\n#### Discovery & OpenAPI\n- **Endpoint**: `/openapi/v3` provides OpenAPI schema for all resources including CRs\n- **Generated**: `pkg/controller/openapi/controller.go` watches CRDs and updates OpenAPI spec\n- **Discovery**: `/api` and `/apis/{group}` endpoints list available resources\n\n---\n\n### 4. **Client-Side Access Layer: Typed Client → Informer → Lister → Dynamic Client**\n\n#### RESTClient Foundation\n- **Location**: `client-go/rest/client.go`\n- **Role**: Low-level HTTP client\n- **Responsibilities**:\n  - Request building (URL, headers, query params)\n  - Authentication (kubeconfig, serviceaccount tokens)\n  - Transport handling (TLS, keep-alive)\n  - Response unmarshaling\n\n#### Typed Client (for CRD Management)\n- **Generated Location**: `client-go/kubernetes/typed/apiextensions/v1/`\n- **Purpose**: Typed access to CustomResourceDefinition resources themselves\n- **Example**: `clientset.ApiextensionsV1().CustomResourceDefinitions().Create(crd)`\n- **Type Safety**: Uses Go structs (CustomResourceDefinition) with compile-time checking\n- **Scheme Usage**: Depends on apimachinery's Scheme for type registration\n\n#### Dynamic Client (for Custom Resources)\n- **Location**: `client-go/dynamic/simple.go`\n- **Wraps**: `rest.Interface` (RESTClient)\n- **Uses**: `Unstructured` type for all objects\n- **Key Properties**:\n  - Schema-less: No compile-time type registration required\n  - GVR-based: Identifies resources by GroupVersionResource, not Scheme\n  - Works immediately with CRs without code generation\n- **Example**:\n  ```go\n  client := dynamic.NewForConfig(config)\n  resource := schema.GroupVersionResource{\n    Group: \"example.com\",\n    Version: \"v1\",\n    Resource: \"widgets\",\n  }\n  widgets := client.Resource(resource).Namespace(\"default\")\n  ```\n\n#### Informers & Listers (Client-Side Cache)\n- **Location**: `client-go/informers/` and `client-go/dynamic/dynamicinformer/`\n- **Purpose**: Local in-memory cache of resources with watch support\n\n##### SharedIndexInformer Flow\n1. **ListWatch Construction** (`dynamicinformer/informer.go:150-176`):\n   - ListFunc: `client.Resource(gvr).Namespace(ns).List(ctx, options)`\n   - WatchFunc: `client.Resource(gvr).Namespace(ns).Watch(ctx, options)`\n   - These funcs delegate to DynamicClient → RESTClient\n\n2. **Caching** (`tools/cache/shared_informer.go`):\n   - Stores objects in thread-safe Indexers\n   - Listwatch periodically lists all objects (resync), watches for changes\n   - Objects cached as Unstructured\n\n3. **Event Handling**:\n   - Informer maintains a queue of Add/Update/Delete events\n   - Calls registered eventhandlers for each event\n   - Indexers updated in response\n\n##### Lister Pattern\n- **Location**: `client-go/dynamic/dynamiclister/`\n- **Purpose**: Query interface over the informer cache\n- **Operations**:\n  - `List(selector)`: Get all objects matching label selector from cache\n  - `Get(name)`: Get single object from cache\n  - `ByNamespace(ns)`: Restrict to specific namespace\n- **Type**: `cache.GenericLister` interface works with Unstructured objects\n\n##### GenericInformer Interface\n- **Location**: `client-go/informers/generic.go`\n- **Definition**:\n  ```go\n  type GenericInformer interface {\n    Informer() cache.SharedIndexInformer\n    Lister() cache.GenericLister\n  }\n  ```\n- **Usage**: Abstracts over typed and dynamic informers with same interface\n\n#### DynamicSharedInformerFactory\n- **Location**: `client-go/dynamic/dynamicinformer/informer.go`\n- **Purpose**: Multiplexes informers for multiple CRDs\n- **Key Method**: `ForResource(gvr)` → `GenericInformer` for that GVR\n- **Lifecycle**:\n  1. Create factory: `NewDynamicSharedInformerFactory(client, resync)`\n  2. Get informer: `factory.ForResource(gvr).Informer()`\n  3. Start: `factory.Start(stopCh)` launches watchers in goroutines\n  4. Wait sync: `factory.WaitForCacheSync(stopCh)` ensures all caches populated\n  5. Query: `factory.ForResource(gvr).Lister().List(selector)`\n\n---\n\n## Analysis: How the Sub-Projects Integrate\n\n### Cross-Project Dependency Flow\n\n```\napimachinery (Foundation)\n    ↓ (defines core types)\n    ├→ Scheme, GVK/GVR, ObjectMeta, Unstructured, Codec interfaces\n\napiextensions-apiserver (Server-Side)\n    ↓ (depends on apimachinery)\n    ├→ Extends Scheme with CustomResourceDefinition types\n    ├→ Uses Unstructured for storing arbitrary CRs\n    ├→ Validates CRs against CRD schemas\n    ├→ Stores in etcd using generic REST storage\n    └→ Serves HTTP endpoints for CRUD + Watch\n\nclient-go (Client-Side)\n    ↓ (depends on apimachinery + apiextensions for CRD types)\n    ├→ Provides RESTClient for HTTP requests\n    ├→ Typed client for CustomResourceDefinition management\n    ├→ Dynamic client for CustomResource access (Unstructured)\n    ├→ Informers for caching with ListWatch\n    └→ Listers for querying cache\n\napi (Hub Types)\n    ↓ (authoritative definitions)\n    └→ Provides standard ObjectMeta, TypeMeta, etc.\n```\n\n### The Bridge: Unstructured Representation\n\nThe critical innovation enabling the CRD architecture:\n1. **On Server**: CRDs define validation via JSONSchemaProps, but CRs stored as Unstructured JSON in etcd\n2. **On Wire**: Objects transmitted as JSON with apiVersion/kind in TypeMeta\n3. **On Client**: DynamicClient receives Unstructured objects, can query/manipulate without schema knowledge\n4. **Checkpoint**: Validation happens server-side before storage, client-side type checking is optional\n\n### Scheme Registration Patterns\n\n**Typed CRD Client**:\n```go\n// In apiextensions-apiserver and client-go\nscheme := runtime.NewScheme()\napiextensionsv1.AddToScheme(scheme)  // Registers CustomResourceDefinition, ConversionReview types\n```\n\n**Dynamic Client**:\n```go\n// In client-go/dynamic\nconfig := ConfigFor(inConfig)\nconfig.NegotiatedSerializer = newBasicNegotiatedSerializer()  // Unstructured codec only\n// No Scheme registration needed!\n```\n\n### The Informer Checkpoint\n\nWhen a DynamicInformer watches a CRD's CRs:\n1. **Server-Side**: customresource_handler watches etcd, returns Unstructured objects\n2. **Over Wire**: HTTP/Watch stream delivers serialized Unstructured (as JSON)\n3. **Client-Side**: DynamicClient deserializes to Unstructured\n4. **In Cache**: Informer's Indexers store Unstructured objects\n5. **Query Time**: Lister returns Unstructured from cache\n\nNo type conversion happens; objects remain Unstructured throughout the pipeline.\n\n### Why Four Sub-Projects?\n\n1. **apimachinery**: Stable foundation used by all Kubernetes code; changes rarely break compatibility\n2. **apiextensions-apiserver**: Can evolve CRD API without breaking apimachinery consumers\n3. **client-go**: Isolated client library; can be vendored independently with different versions than apiserver\n4. **api**: Core and extension API definitions; separates type definitions from implementation\n\nEach project has its own versioning, release cycle, and import constraints. This modularity allows:\n- Users can update client-go independently from their cluster version\n- New CRD features don't require updating all consumers\n- Clear separation of concerns: definitions (api) vs runtime (apiextensions) vs client libraries (client-go)\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\nA Custom Resource Definition flows through the Kubernetes ecosystem as follows:\n\n**Server-side**: A CRD type definition (stored as a CustomResourceDefinition object) is validated, stored in etcd, and served via the dynamic HTTP handler which creates a REST endpoint for custom resources. The endpoint validates CRs against the CRD's schema and stores them as Unstructured JSON objects in etcd.\n\n**Client-side**: Clients access custom resources via the DynamicClient, which performs HTTP requests to the CRD-provided endpoints. A DynamicSharedInformerFactory establishes ListWatch connections, caching Unstructured objects locally and maintaining an in-memory index. Listers query this cache using the GenericInformer interface, enabling operator code to efficiently watch and react to CR changes without compile-time knowledge of the resource type.\n\n**Cross-cutting**: The Scheme registry in apimachinery ties typed and untyped clients together, while GroupVersionKind/GroupVersionResource identifiers enable routing and discovery. The Unstructured type allows the entire pipeline to work with schema-less objects, making CRDs extensible without requiring code generation for each new resource type.\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
